<script>
const CURRENCY = <?= json_encode(CURRENCY) ?>;
const SITE_URL = <?= json_encode(SITE_URL) ?>;
const SITE_NAME = <?= json_encode(SITE_NAME) ?>;

let currentUser = <?= json_encode($initUser ? [
    'id' => (int)$initUser['id'],
    'name' => $initUser['name'],
    'email' => $initUser['email'],
    'balance' => (float)$initUser['balance'],
    'api_status' => $initUser['api_status'],
    'api_key' => $initUser['api_status'] === 'approved' ? $initUser['api_key'] : null
] : null) ?>;

let categories = [];
let products   = [];
let activeCat  = 'all';
let authMode   = 'login';

let payConfig = <?= json_encode([
    'bkash_status'   => isPaymentMethodEnabled($settings['bkash_status'] ?? '1'),
    'bkash_number'   => $settings['bkash_number'] ?? '',
    'nagad_status'   => isPaymentMethodEnabled($settings['nagad_status'] ?? '1'),
    'nagad_number'   => $settings['nagad_number'] ?? '',
    'binance_status' => isPaymentMethodEnabled($settings['binance_status'] ?? '1'),
    'binance_id'     => $settings['binance_id'] ?? '',
    'binance_rate'   => (float)($settings['binance_rate'] ?? 120),
    'min_deposit'    => (float)($settings['min_deposit'] ?? 50),
]) ?>;

document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
  const btn = e.target.closest('.btn, .menu-btn, .cat-chip, .dock-btn, .d-item');
  if (!btn) return;
  const r = btn.getBoundingClientRect();
  const sp = document.createElement('span');
  const sz = Math.max(r.width, r.height);
  sp.style.width = sp.style.height = sz + 'px';
  sp.style.left = (e.clientX - r.left - sz / 2) + 'px';
  sp.style.top  = (e.clientY - r.top - sz / 2) + 'px';
  sp.className  = 'ripple-effect';
  btn.appendChild(sp);
  setTimeout(() => sp.remove(), 500);
});

function startLoad() { document.getElementById('topLoader').className = 'top-loader active'; }
function endLoad() {
  const l = document.getElementById('topLoader');
  l.className = 'top-loader done';
  setTimeout(() => l.className = 'top-loader', 280);
}

function syncPageFromUrl() {
  const hash = location.hash.replace('#', '').trim();
  const validPages = ['shop', 'otp', 'orders', 'deposit', 'profile', 'api-doc'];
  if (hash && validPages.includes(hash) && hash !== 'shop') {
    if (['orders', 'deposit', 'profile'].includes(hash) && !currentUser) {
      switchPage('shop');
    } else {
      switchPage(hash, false);
    }
  } else {
    switchPage('shop', false);
  }
}

window.addEventListener('hashchange', syncPageFromUrl);

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  document.getElementById('splashScreen').classList.add('hide');
  renderNavAuth();
  renderPaymentGatewayUI();
  syncPageFromUrl();
  if (location.hash.replace('#', '').trim() && location.hash.replace('#', '').trim() !== 'shop') {
    loadCatalog();
  }

  // Instant Live Sync Function (Updates Categories, Stock, Balance, Deposits & Profile)
  async function liveSyncUserPanel() {
    if (document.hidden) return;
    await loadCatalog();
    const activeSec = document.querySelector('.sec.active');
    if (activeSec && currentUser) {
      if (activeSec.id === 'sec-deposit') loadDeposits();
      if (activeSec.id === 'sec-orders') loadOrders();
      if (activeSec.id === 'sec-profile') loadProfile();
    }
  }

  // 1. Sync immediately the moment you switch back to the User tab!
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
    if (!document.hidden) liveSyncUserPanel();
  });
  window.addEventListener('focus', liveSyncUserPanel);

  // 2. Also sync automatically every 15 seconds while watching (Saves Server CPU)!
  setInterval(liveSyncUserPanel, 15000);
});

async function manualReloadStore() {
  const ico = document.getElementById('reloadIco');
  ico.classList.add('spin-anim');
  startLoad();
  await loadCatalog();
  const activeSec = document.querySelector('.sec.active');
  if (activeSec && currentUser) {
    if (activeSec.id === 'sec-deposit') await loadDeposits();
    if (activeSec.id === 'sec-orders') await loadOrders();
    if (activeSec.id === 'sec-profile') await loadProfile();
  }
  endLoad();
  setTimeout(() => ico.classList.remove('spin-anim'), 500);
  showToast('Store & wallet synced!');
}

function toggleDrawer(open) {
  document.getElementById('drawer').classList.toggle('open', open);
  document.getElementById('drawerOv').classList.toggle('open', open);
}

function showToast(msg, isErr = false) {
  const t = document.getElementById('toast');
  t.style.background = isErr ? '#e11d48' : '#0f172a';
  t.innerHTML = `<i class="${isErr ? 'ri-error-warning-line' : 'ri-checkbox-circle-line'}"></i> ` + esc(msg);
  t.classList.add('show');
  setTimeout(() => t.classList.remove('show'), 3000);
}

function switchPage(id, updateUrl = true) {
  if (updateUrl) {
    if (id === 'shop') {
      history.pushState(null, null, window.location.pathname);
    } else {
      history.pushState(null, null, '#' + id);
    }
  }
  startLoad();
  document.querySelectorAll('.sec').forEach(s => s.classList.remove('active'));
  document.querySelectorAll('.nav-link, .dock-btn, .d-item').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));

  const sec = document.getElementById('sec-' + id);
  if (sec) sec.classList.add('active');

  ['tab-', 'm-', 'd-'].forEach(prefix => {
    const el = document.getElementById(prefix + id);
    if (el) el.classList.add('active');
  });

  if (id === 'shop') loadCatalog();
  if (id === 'orders') loadOrders();
  if (id === 'deposit') { loadDeposits(); loadCatalog(); }
  if (id === 'profile') loadProfile();
  if (id === 'api-doc') updateDocApiKeys();
  window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
  setTimeout(endLoad, 220);
}

function updateDocApiKeys() {
  const key = (currentUser && currentUser.api_key) ? currentUser.api_key : 'YOUR_API_KEY';
  document.querySelectorAll('.doc-key').forEach(el => el.textContent = key);
}

function openProtectedPage(id) {
  toggleDrawer(false);
  if (!currentUser) return openAuthModal('login');
  switchPage(id);
}

function openProfilePage() {
  toggleDrawer(false);
  if (!currentUser) return openAuthModal('login');
  switchPage('profile');
}

function renderNavAuth() {
  const box = document.getElementById('navAuthBox');
  const dLogout = document.getElementById('drawerLogoutBtn');
  if (!currentUser) {
    if (dLogout) dLogout.style.display = 'none';
    box.innerHTML = `
      <button class="btn btn-outline" onclick="openAuthModal('login')"><i class="ri-login-circle-line"></i> Sign In</button>
      <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="openAuthModal('register')"><i class="ri-user-add-line"></i> Join</button>
    `;
  } else {
    if (dLogout) dLogout.style.display = 'inline-flex';
    box.innerHTML = `
      <div class="bal-pill" onclick="openProtectedPage('deposit')" title="Add Balance">
        <i class="ri-wallet-3-fill"></i> ${CURRENCY}${Number(currentUser.balance).toFixed(2)}
      </div>
    `;
  }
  updateDocApiKeys();
}

function getCatIconHtml(ico, color = '') {
  const cls = (ico && ico.startsWith('ri-')) ? ico.replace('-line', '-fill') : 'ri-mail-star-fill';
  const st = color ? ` style="color:${color}"` : '';
  return `<i class="${esc(cls)}"${st}></i>`;
}

const CAT_PALETTES = [
  { color: '#0284c7', bg: '#f0f9ff', border: '#bae6fd' },
  { color: '#e11d48', bg: '#fff1f2', border: '#fecdd3' },
  { color: '#7c3aed', bg: '#f5f3ff', border: '#ddd6fe' },
  { color: '#059669', bg: '#ecfdf5', border: '#a7f3d0' },
  { color: '#d97706', bg: '#fffbeb', border: '#fde68a' }
];

let lastCatalogSignature = '';
let lastUserSignature    = '';

async function loadCatalog() {
  try {
    const res  = await fetch('api.php?action=web_catalog&_t=' + Date.now(), { cache: 'no-store' });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (!data.success) return;

    // 1. Only update top balance/user bar if user balance or status actually changed
    if (data.user !== undefined) {
      const newUserSig = JSON.stringify(data.user);
      if (newUserSig !== lastUserSignature) {
        lastUserSignature = newUserSig;
        currentUser = data.user;
        renderNavAuth();
      }
    }

    // 1.5 Sync Payment Gateway Config (ON/OFF methods, numbers, USDT rate)
    if (data.payment_config) {
      payConfig = data.payment_config;
      renderPaymentGatewayUI();
    }

    // 2. Only re-render categories & product boxes if stock, price, or products actually changed!
    const newCatSig = JSON.stringify({ c: data.categories, p: data.products, s: data.total_stock });
    if (newCatSig === lastCatalogSignature) {
      return; // Nothing changed -> Do NOT touch DOM or jump!
    }
    lastCatalogSignature = newCatSig;

    if (activeCat !== 'all' && !(data.categories || []).some(c => c.slug === activeCat)) {
      activeCat = 'all';
    }

    categories = data.categories || [];
    products   = data.products || [];

    document.getElementById('stProdCount').textContent  = products.length;
    document.getElementById('stStockCount').textContent = Number(data.total_stock || 0).toLocaleString();

    renderCategories();
    renderProducts();
  } catch (e) {}
}

function renderCategories() {
  const bar = document.getElementById('catBar');
  let html = `<button class="cat-chip ${activeCat === 'all' ? 'active' : ''}" onclick="filterCat('all', this)"><i class="ri-apps-2-fill"></i> All</button>`;
  categories.forEach((c, idx) => {
    const act = activeCat === c.slug ? 'active' : '';
    const pal = CAT_PALETTES[idx % CAT_PALETTES.length];
    const activeStyle = act ? `style="background:${pal.color};border-color:${pal.color};color:#fff"` : '';
    html += `<button class="cat-chip ${act}" ${activeStyle} onclick="filterCat('${esc(c.slug)}', this)">${getCatIconHtml(c.icon, act ? '#fff' : pal.color)} ${esc(c.name)}</button>`;
  });
  bar.innerHTML = html;
}

function filterCat(slug, btn) {
  activeCat = slug;
  renderCategories();
  renderProducts();
}

function renderProducts() {
  const list = document.getElementById('prodList');
  const filtered = activeCat === 'all' ? products : products.filter(p => p.category === activeCat);

  if (!filtered.length) {
    list.innerHTML = `<div class="card"><div class="card-body" style="text-align:center;color:var(--muted);padding:24px"><i class="ri-inbox-2-fill" style="font-size:24px;color:var(--primary)"></i><div style="margin-top:6px;font-size:12px;font-weight:700">No products in this category</div></div></div>`;
    return;
  }

  const catMap = {};
  const catIdxMap = {};
  categories.forEach((c, i) => {
    catMap[c.slug] = c;
    catIdxMap[c.slug] = i;
  });

  list.innerHTML = filtered.map((p, idx) => {
    const c = catMap[p.category] || { name: p.category, icon: 'ri-box-3-fill' };
    const cIdx = catIdxMap[p.category] !== undefined ? catIdxMap[p.category] : idx;
    const pal = CAT_PALETTES[cIdx % CAT_PALETTES.length];
    const stk = Number(p.stock);
    const stkCls = stk === 0 ? 'stk-out' : (stk < 20 ? 'stk-low' : 'stk-ok');
    const stkTxt = stk === 0 ? 'Out of Stock' : `Stock: ${stk}`;

    return `
      <div class="p-card" style="animation-delay:${Math.min(idx * 0.04, 0.35)}s">
        <div class="p-left">
          <div class="p-ico" style="background:${pal.bg};border-color:${pal.border};color:${pal.color}">${getCatIconHtml(c.icon, pal.color)}</div>
          <div class="p-info">
            <div class="p-name">${esc(p.name)}</div>
            <div class="p-meta">
              <span class="stk-tag ${stkCls}"><i class="ri-checkbox-blank-circle-fill" style="font-size:6px"></i> ${stkTxt}</span>
              ${p.description ? `<span class="p-desc">${esc(p.description)}</span>` : ''}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="p-right">
          <div class="p-price">
            <span class="val">${CURRENCY}${Number(p.price).toFixed(2)}</span>
            <span class="lbl">per pc</span>
          </div>
          <button class="btn ${stk > 0 ? 'btn-primary' : 'btn-ghost'}" style="padding:7px 13px" onclick="openBuyPopup(${p.id})" ${stk === 0 ? 'disabled' : ''}>
            <i class="${stk > 0 ? 'ri-shopping-cart-2-fill' : 'ri-forbid-line'}"></i> ${stk > 0 ? 'Buy' : 'Sold'}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }).join('');
}

let selectedBuyProd = null;

function openBuyPopup(prodId) {
  if (!currentUser) return openAuthModal('login');
  const p = products.find(x => Number(x.id) === Number(prodId));
  if (!p || Number(p.stock) <= 0) return showToast('Product is out of stock!', true);

  selectedBuyProd = p;
  document.getElementById('bpTitle').textContent      = p.name;
  document.getElementById('bpUnitPrice').textContent  = CURRENCY + Number(p.price).toFixed(2) + ' / pc';
  document.getElementById('bpStockBadge').textContent = p.stock + ' pcs available';

  const fullText = (p.full_description && p.full_description.trim() !== '')
    ? p.full_description
    : (p.description && p.description.trim() !== '' ? p.description : '• Instant automated delivery right after purchase.\n• Make sure to read all rules before buying.');
  document.getElementById('bpFullDesc').textContent = fullText;

  const qInput = document.getElementById('bpQtyInput');
  qInput.value = 1;
  qInput.max   = p.stock;
  updateBuyTotal();

  document.getElementById('buyPopupModal').classList.add('show');
}

function stepBuyQty(delta) {
  if (!selectedBuyProd) return;
  const qInput = document.getElementById('bpQtyInput');
  let val = parseInt(qInput.value || '1', 10) + delta;
  if (isNaN(val) || val < 1) val = 1;
  if (val > Number(selectedBuyProd.stock)) val = Number(selectedBuyProd.stock);
  qInput.value = val;
  updateBuyTotal();
}

function updateBuyTotal() {
  if (!selectedBuyProd) return;
  const qInput = document.getElementById('bpQtyInput');
  let val = parseInt(qInput.value, 10);
  if (isNaN(val) || val < 1) val = 1;
  if (val > Number(selectedBuyProd.stock)) {
    val = Number(selectedBuyProd.stock);
    qInput.value = val;
  }
  const total = val * Number(selectedBuyProd.price);
  document.getElementById('bpTotalPayable').textContent = CURRENCY + total.toFixed(2);
}

async function confirmBuyFromPopup() {
  if (!selectedBuyProd) return;
  const qty = parseInt(document.getElementById('bpQtyInput').value || '1', 10) || 1;
  const btn = document.getElementById('btnConfirmBuyPopup');
  const oldHtml = btn.innerHTML;
  btn.disabled = true;
  btn.innerHTML = '<i class="ri-loader-4-line spin-anim"></i> Processing...';
  startLoad();

  const fd = new FormData();
  fd.append('action', 'buy');
  fd.append('slug', selectedBuyProd.slug);
  fd.append('qty', qty);

  try {
    const res  = await fetch('api.php', { method: 'POST', body: fd });
    const data = await res.json();
    endLoad();
    btn.disabled = false;
    btn.innerHTML = oldHtml;

    if (!data.success) return showToast(data.error, true);

    closeModal('buyPopupModal');
    showToast(`Purchased ${data.qty}x ${data.product}!`);
    currentUser.balance = data.new_balance;
    renderNavAuth();
    lastCatalogSignature = ''; // Force immediate stock refresh
    loadCatalog();
    openDeliveryModal(data.order_code, data.items);
  } catch (e) {
    endLoad();
    btn.disabled = false;
    btn.innerHTML = oldHtml;
    showToast('Network error! Please try again.', true);
  }
}

function openDeliveryModal(code, items) {
  document.getElementById('mdlOrderCode').textContent = code;
  document.getElementById('mdlItems').value = (items || []).join('\n');
  document.getElementById('mdlTxt').href = `api.php?action=download_order&order_code=${encodeURIComponent(code)}&format=txt`;
  document.getElementById('mdlCsv').href = `api.php?action=download_order&order_code=${encodeURIComponent(code)}&format=csv`;
  document.getElementById('orderModal').classList.add('show');
}

function copyDelivered() {
  const el = document.getElementById('mdlItems');
  el.select();
  navigator.clipboard.writeText(el.value);
  showToast('Copied all accounts!');
}

function openAuthModal(mode) {
  setAuthMode(mode);
  document.getElementById('authErrorBox').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('authModal').classList.add('show');
}
function closeModal(id) { document.getElementById(id).classList.remove('show'); }
function setAuthMode(mode) {
  authMode = mode;
  document.getElementById('nameField').style.display = mode === 'register' ? 'block' : 'none';
  document.getElementById('authTitle').innerHTML = mode === 'register' ? '<i class="ri-user-add-line"></i> Create Account' : '<i class="ri-shield-user-line"></i> Sign In';
  document.getElementById('btnTabLogin').className = mode === 'login' ? 'btn btn-primary' : 'btn btn-ghost';
  document.getElementById('btnTabReg').className   = mode === 'register' ? 'btn btn-primary' : 'btn btn-ghost';
  document.getElementById('authErrorBox').style.display = 'none';
}

async function submitAuthForm(e) {
  e.preventDefault();
  const errBox = document.getElementById('authErrorBox');
  const btn    = document.getElementById('btnAuthGo');
  errBox.style.display = 'none';

  const fd = new FormData();
  fd.append('action', authMode);
  if (authMode === 'register') fd.append('name', document.getElementById('authName').value.trim());
  fd.append('email', document.getElementById('authEmail').value.trim());
  fd.append('password', document.getElementById('authPass').value);

  btn.disabled = true;
  btn.innerHTML = '<i class="ri-loader-4-line spin-anim"></i> Processing...';
  startLoad();

  try {
    const res  = await fetch('api.php', { method: 'POST', body: fd });
    const data = await res.json();
    endLoad();
    btn.disabled = false;
    btn.innerHTML = '<i class="ri-arrow-right-circle-line"></i> Continue';

    if (!data.success) {
      errBox.textContent = data.error || 'Authentication failed';
      errBox.style.display = 'block';
      return;
    }

    currentUser = data.user;
    renderNavAuth();
    closeModal('authModal');
    showToast(data.message);
  } catch (err) {
    endLoad();
    btn.disabled = false;
    btn.innerHTML = '<i class="ri-arrow-right-circle-line"></i> Continue';
    errBox.textContent = 'Network error occurred.';
    errBox.style.display = 'block';
  }
}

async function handleGoogleCredential(response) {
  if (!response || !response.credential) return;
  startLoad();
  const fd = new FormData();
  fd.append('action', 'google_login');
  fd.append('credential', response.credential);

  try {
    const res  = await fetch('api.php', { method: 'POST', body: fd });
    const data = await res.json();
    endLoad();
    if (!data.success) {
      const errBox = document.getElementById('authErrorBox');
      errBox.textContent = data.error || 'Google Sign-In failed';
      errBox.style.display = 'block';
      return;
    }
    currentUser = data.user;
    renderNavAuth();
    closeModal('authModal');
    showToast(data.message || 'Signed in with Google!');
  } catch (e) {
    endLoad();
    showToast('Network error during Google Sign-In', true);
  }
}

async function doLogout() {
  startLoad();
  const fd = new FormData(); fd.append('action', 'logout');
  await fetch('api.php', { method: 'POST', body: fd });
  endLoad();
  currentUser = null;
  renderNavAuth();
  switchPage('shop');
  showToast('Logged out.');
}

async function readMailOtp() {
  const line = document.getElementById('otpInput').value.trim();
  const btn  = document.getElementById('btnOtp');
  const box  = document.getElementById('otpResult');
  if (!line) return showToast('Paste account line first!', true);

  btn.disabled = true;
  btn.innerHTML = '<i class="ri-loader-4-line spin-anim"></i> Checking Inbox...';
  box.style.display = 'none';
  startLoad();

  const fd = new FormData();
  fd.append('action', 'get_otp');
  fd.append('web_client', '1');
  fd.append('account', line);

  try {
    const res  = await fetch('api.php', { method: 'POST', body: fd });
    const data = await res.json();
    endLoad();
    box.style.display = 'block';

    if (!data.success) {
      box.innerHTML = `<div style="background:var(--danger-bg);color:var(--danger);padding:9px 11px;border-radius:8px;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:700"><i class="ri-error-warning-line"></i> ${esc(data.error)}</div>`;
    } else {
      let html = '';
      if (data.latest_otp) {
        html += `
          <div style="background:var(--primary-light);border:2px dashed var(--primary);padding:12px;border-radius:10px;text-align:center;margin-bottom:10px">
            <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:800;color:var(--primary)">LATEST OTP CODE</div>
            <div class="mono" style="font-size:24px;font-weight:800;color:var(--primary-dark);margin:4px 0">${esc(data.latest_otp)}</div>
            <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="navigator.clipboard.writeText('${esc(data.latest_otp)}');showToast('OTP Copied!')"><i class="ri-file-copy-line"></i> Copy Code</button>
          </div>`;
      }
      if (data.messages && data.messages.length) {
        html += data.messages.map(m => `
          <div style="border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:8px;padding:9px;margin-bottom:6px">
            <div style="font-weight:800;font-size:12px">${esc(m.subject)}</div>
            <div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--primary);margin:2px 0">${esc(m.from)} • ${esc(m.date)}</div>
            <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--muted)">${esc(m.preview)}</div>
          </div>`).join('');
      } else {
        html += `<div style="text-align:center;color:var(--muted);font-size:11.5px">Inbox is empty.</div>`;
      }
      box.innerHTML = html;
    }
  } catch (e) { endLoad(); }
  btn.disabled = false;
  btn.innerHTML = '<i class="ri-search-eye-line"></i> Check Inbox & Get Code';
}

async function loadOrders() {
  const res  = await fetch('api.php?action=orders');
  const data = await res.json();
  const body = document.getElementById('ordersBody');
  if (!data.success || !data.orders.length) {
    body.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="6" style="text-align:center;color:var(--muted)">No orders yet.</td></tr>`;
    return;
  }
  window.myOrders = data.orders;
  body.innerHTML = data.orders.map((o, i) => `
    <tr>
      <td class="mono" style="font-weight:800;color:var(--primary)">${esc(o.order_code)}</td>
      <td><b>${esc(o.product)}</b></td>
      <td>${o.qty}</td>
      <td><b>${CURRENCY}${Number(o.total_price).toFixed(2)}</b></td>
      <td style="font-size:10.5px;color:var(--muted)">${esc(o.created_at)}</td>
      <td><button class="btn btn-outline" onclick="openDeliveryModal(window.myOrders[${i}].order_code, window.myOrders[${i}].items)"><i class="ri-eye-line"></i> View</button></td>
    </tr>
  `).join('');
}

function isMethodOn(v) {
  return v !== false && v !== 0 && v !== '0' && String(v).toLowerCase() !== 'off' && String(v).toLowerCase() !== 'false';
}

function renderPaymentGatewayUI(selectedOverride) {
  const tabsEl = document.getElementById('payMethodTabs');
  if (!tabsEl) return;

  const methods = [
    { key: 'bKash', label: 'bKash', active: isMethodOn(payConfig.bkash_status), num: payConfig.bkash_number || '', color: '#e2136e', grad: 'linear-gradient(135deg,#e2136e,#b80d57)', icon: 'ri-smartphone-line' },
    { key: 'Nagad', label: 'Nagad', active: isMethodOn(payConfig.nagad_status), num: payConfig.nagad_number || '', color: '#f7941d', grad: 'linear-gradient(135deg,#f7941d,#d9531e)', icon: 'ri-smartphone-line' },
    { key: 'Binance', label: 'Binance', active: isMethodOn(payConfig.binance_status), num: payConfig.binance_id || '', color: '#d97706', grad: 'linear-gradient(135deg,#1e2329,#3b424c)', icon: 'ri-bit-coin-line' }
  ].filter(m => m.active);

  const boxEl   = document.getElementById('payGatewayBox');
  const formEl  = document.getElementById('payFormFields');
  const calcEl  = document.getElementById('binanceCalcBox');
  const curInput = document.getElementById('depMethod');

  if (!methods.length) {
    tabsEl.innerHTML = `<div style="grid-column:span 3;padding:10px;background:var(--danger-bg);color:var(--danger);border-radius:8px;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:700;text-align:center"><i class="ri-error-warning-line"></i> All payment methods are currently turned OFF by Admin.</div>`;
    if (boxEl) boxEl.style.display = 'none';
    if (formEl) formEl.style.display = 'none';
    if (calcEl) calcEl.style.display = 'none';
    return;
  }

  if (boxEl) boxEl.style.display = 'block';
  if (formEl) formEl.style.display = 'block';

  let current = selectedOverride || (curInput ? curInput.value : 'bKash');
  if (!methods.some(m => m.key === current)) {
    current = methods[0].key;
  }
  if (curInput) curInput.value = current;

  tabsEl.innerHTML = methods.map(m => {
    const isSel = m.key === current;
    return `
      <button type="button" onclick="renderPaymentGatewayUI('${m.key}')" style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:5px;padding:8px 6px;border-radius:9px;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:800;cursor:pointer;transition:.15s;border:${isSel ? '2px solid ' + m.color : '1px solid var(--border)'};background:${isSel ? '#fff' : '#f8fafc'};color:${isSel ? m.color : 'var(--muted)'};box-shadow:${isSel ? '0 3px 10px rgba(0,0,0,0.06)' : 'none'}">
        <i class="${m.icon}"></i> ${m.label}
      </button>
    `;
  }).join('');

  const sel = methods.find(m => m.key === current) || methods[0];
  if (boxEl) {
    boxEl.style.background = sel.grad;
    document.getElementById('pgMethodTitle').textContent = sel.key === 'Binance' ? 'Binance Pay (Auto-API Verify)' : `${sel.label} Personal (Send Money)`;
    document.getElementById('pgMinDep').textContent      = payConfig.min_deposit || 50;
    document.getElementById('pgNumLabel').textContent    = sel.key === 'Binance' ? 'Binance Pay ID / UID' : `${sel.label} Personal Number`;
    document.getElementById('pgTargetNum').textContent   = sel.num || 'Not Configured';
    document.getElementById('pgStepHint').textContent    = sel.key === 'Binance'
      ? `Send USDT via Binance Pay to the ID above (Rate: 1 USDT = ${CURRENCY}${payConfig.binance_rate || 120}). Enter your Binance Order ID / TrxID & Screenshot below.`
      : `Send Money to the ${sel.label} number above. Then enter your Sender Number, Amount, TrxID & Payment Screenshot below.`;
  }

  if (calcEl) {
    calcEl.style.display = sel.key === 'Binance' ? 'block' : 'none';
    const rateEl = document.getElementById('lblUsdtRate');
    if (rateEl) rateEl.textContent = payConfig.binance_rate || 120;
  }

  const lblSender = document.getElementById('lblDepSender');
  const inpSender = document.getElementById('depSender');
  const lblTrx    = document.getElementById('lblDepTrx');
  const inpTrx    = document.getElementById('depTrx');

  if (sel.key === 'Binance') {
    if (lblSender) lblSender.textContent = 'Your Binance Name / Pay ID';
    if (inpSender) inpSender.placeholder = 'e.g. Binance UID or Name';
    if (lblTrx) lblTrx.textContent       = 'Binance Order ID / TrxID';
    if (inpTrx) inpTrx.placeholder       = 'e.g. 29384756102938';
  } else {
    if (lblSender) lblSender.textContent = `Sender ${sel.label} Number`;
    if (inpSender) inpSender.placeholder = '017XXXXXXXX';
    if (lblTrx) lblTrx.textContent       = `${sel.label} Transaction ID (TrxID)`;
    if (inpTrx) inpTrx.placeholder       = 'e.g. BHG7654321';
  }
}

function copyGatewayNum() {
  const txt = document.getElementById('pgTargetNum').textContent.trim();
  if (!txt) return;
  navigator.clipboard.writeText(txt);
  showToast('Payment ID / Number Copied!');
}

function calcUsdtToBdt() {
  const usdt = parseFloat(document.getElementById('depUsdtInput').value || '0');
  const rate = parseFloat(payConfig.binance_rate || 120);
  const bdt  = Math.round(usdt * rate * 100) / 100;
  document.getElementById('lblConvertedBdt').textContent = CURRENCY + (bdt > 0 ? bdt.toFixed(2) : '0.00');
  if (bdt > 0) {
    document.getElementById('depAmount').value = bdt;
  }
}

function handleScreenshotSelect(input) {
  const file = input.files && input.files[0];
  if (!file) return;
  if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) {
    showToast('Please select a valid image screenshot!', true);
    input.value = '';
    return;
  }
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = function(e) {
    const img = new Image();
    img.onload = function() {
      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
      let w = img.width;
      let h = img.height;
      const maxDim = 650;
      if (w > maxDim || h > maxDim) {
        if (w > h) { h = Math.round(h * (maxDim / w)); w = maxDim; }
        else { w = Math.round(w * (maxDim / h)); h = maxDim; }
      }
      canvas.width = w;
      canvas.height = h;
      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, w, h);
      const compressedDataUrl = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.72);
      document.getElementById('depScreenData').value = compressedDataUrl;
      document.getElementById('depScreenPreviewImg').src = compressedDataUrl;
      document.getElementById('depScreenPreviewWrap').style.display = 'block';
    };
    img.src = e.target.result;
  };
  reader.readAsDataURL(file);
}

function clearDepScreenshot() {
  document.getElementById('depScreenFile').value = '';
  document.getElementById('depScreenData').value = '';
  document.getElementById('depScreenPreviewImg').src = '';
  document.getElementById('depScreenPreviewWrap').style.display = 'none';
}

async function submitDeposit(ev) {
  const btn = (ev && ev.currentTarget) ? ev.currentTarget : document.getElementById('btnSubmitDep');
  if (btn && btn.disabled) return;

  const method     = document.getElementById('depMethod').value;
  const senderNum  = document.getElementById('depSender').value.trim();
  const amount     = document.getElementById('depAmount').value.trim();
  const trxId      = document.getElementById('depTrx').value.trim();
  const screenData = document.getElementById('depScreenData').value.trim();

  if (!senderNum || !amount || !trxId) {
    return showToast('Please fill Sender Number, Amount and TrxID!', true);
  }
  if (!screenData) {
    return showToast('Please upload your Payment Screenshot!', true);
  }

  const oldHtml = btn ? btn.innerHTML : '';
  if (btn) { btn.disabled = true; btn.innerHTML = '<i class="ri-loader-4-line spin-anim"></i> Submitting & Checking...'; }
  startLoad();

  const fd = new FormData();
  fd.append('action', 'deposit');
  fd.append('method', method);
  fd.append('sender_num', senderNum);
  fd.append('amount', amount);
  fd.append('trx_id', trxId);
  fd.append('screenshot_data', screenData);

  try {
    const res  = await fetch('api.php', { method: 'POST', body: fd });
    const data = await res.json();
    endLoad();
    if (btn) { btn.disabled = false; btn.innerHTML = oldHtml; }
    if (!data.success) return showToast(data.error, true);
    showToast(data.message);
    document.getElementById('depTrx').value = '';
    document.getElementById('depAmount').value = '';
    if (document.getElementById('depUsdtInput')) document.getElementById('depUsdtInput').value = '';
    clearDepScreenshot();
    loadDeposits();
    loadCatalog(); // Sync balance if auto-approved immediately!
  } catch (e) {
    endLoad();
    if (btn) { btn.disabled = false; btn.innerHTML = oldHtml; }
    showToast('Weak network connection! Please try again.', true);
  }
}

async function loadDeposits() {
  renderPaymentGatewayUI();
  const res  = await fetch('api.php?action=deposits&_t=' + Date.now(), { cache: 'no-store' });
  const data = await res.json();
  const body = document.getElementById('depBody');
  if (!data.success || !data.deposits.length) {
    body.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="4" style="text-align:center;color:var(--muted)">No deposits yet.</td></tr>`;
    return;
  }
  body.innerHTML = data.deposits.map(d => {
    const cls = d.status === 'approved' ? 'b-ok' : (d.status === 'rejected' ? 'b-err' : 'b-warn');
    const statusText = d.status === 'pending'
      ? 'PENDING (Wait few minutes for approval)'
      : d.status.toUpperCase();
    return `
      <tr>
        <td><b>${esc(d.method)}</b></td>
        <td><b>${CURRENCY}${Number(d.amount).toFixed(2)}</b></td>
        <td class="mono">${esc(d.trx_id)}</td>
        <td><span class="badge ${cls}">${esc(statusText)}</span></td>
      </tr>`;
  }).join('');
}

async function loadProfile() {
  const res  = await fetch('api.php?action=me');
  const data = await res.json();
  if (data.success) { currentUser = data; renderNavAuth(); }
  document.getElementById('profName').value  = currentUser.name || '';
  document.getElementById('profEmail').value = currentUser.email || '';

  const badge = document.getElementById('apiBadge');
  const box   = document.getElementById('apiControlBox');
  const st    = currentUser.api_status || 'none';

  if (st === 'approved') {
    badge.className = 'badge b-ok'; badge.textContent = 'APPROVED';
    box.innerHTML = `
      <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap">
        <input type="text" readonly class="mono" id="myKeyField" value="${esc(currentUser.api_key || '')}" style="flex:1;padding:7px 10px;border-radius:8px;border:1px solid var(--primary-border);background:var(--primary-light);font-weight:700;color:var(--primary-dark);font-size:11.5px">
        <button class="btn btn-outline" onclick="navigator.clipboard.writeText(document.getElementById('myKeyField').value);showToast('API Key Copied!')"><i class="ri-file-copy-line"></i> Copy</button>
        <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="regenKey()"><i class="ri-refresh-line"></i> New Key</button>
      </div>`;
  } else if (st === 'pending') {
    badge.className = 'badge b-warn'; badge.textContent = 'PENDING APPROVAL';
    box.innerHTML = `<div style="font-size:12px;color:#b45309"><i class="ri-time-line"></i> Your API Key request is pending Admin approval.</div>`;
  } else if (st === 'revoked') {
    badge.className = 'badge b-err'; badge.textContent = 'REVOKED';
    box.innerHTML = `<div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--danger)"><i class="ri-forbid-line"></i> Your API access was revoked by Admin.</div>`;
  } else {
    badge.className = 'badge b-blue'; badge.textContent = 'NOT REQUESTED';
    box.innerHTML = `
      <p style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--muted);margin-bottom:8px">Request Reseller API Key access to connect your Telegram bot or external website.</p>
      <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="reqApi()"><i class="ri-rocket-line"></i> Request API Access</button>`;
  }
}

async function saveProfile() {
  const fd = new FormData();
  fd.append('action', 'update_profile');
  fd.append('name', document.getElementById('profName').value.trim());
  fd.append('email', document.getElementById('profEmail').value.trim());
  const r = await fetch('api.php', { method: 'POST', body: fd });
  const d = await r.json();
  if (!d.success) return showToast(d.error, true);
  currentUser.name = d.name; currentUser.email = d.email;
  renderNavAuth(); showToast(d.message);
}

async function savePassword() {
  const fd = new FormData();
  fd.append('action', 'change_password');
  fd.append('current_password', document.getElementById('profCurPass').value);
  fd.append('new_password', document.getElementById('profNewPass').value);
  const r = await fetch('api.php', { method: 'POST', body: fd });
  const d = await r.json();
  if (!d.success) return showToast(d.error, true);
  document.getElementById('profCurPass').value = '';
  document.getElementById('profNewPass').value = '';
  showToast(d.message);
}

async function reqApi() {
  const fd = new FormData(); fd.append('action', 'request_api');
  const r = await fetch('api.php', { method: 'POST', body: fd });
  const d = await r.json();
  if (!d.success) return showToast(d.error, true);
  showToast(d.message); loadProfile();
}

async function regenKey() {
  const fd = new FormData(); fd.append('action', 'regenerate_api');
  const r = await fetch('api.php', { method: 'POST', body: fd });
  const d = await r.json();
  if (!d.success) return showToast(d.error, true);
  showToast(d.message); loadProfile();
}

function esc(s) {
  return String(s ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, m => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[m]));
}
</script>
</body>
</html>

$loginError = '';
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['admin_login'])) {
    $u = trim($_POST['username'] ?? '');
    $p = trim($_POST['password'] ?? '');
    if ($u === ADMIN_USER && $p === ADMIN_PASS) {
        $_SESSION['admin_logged_in'] = true;
        header('Location: admin.php');
        exit;
    }
    $loginError = 'Invalid Admin username or password!';
}

// ============================================================
//  ADMIN AJAX API HANDLER
// ============================================================
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['admin_action'])) {
    requireAdmin();
    if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_ACTIVE) session_write_close();
    if (function_exists('clearStoreCache')) clearStoreCache();
    $act = $_POST['admin_action'];

    // 1. CATEGORIES
    if ($act === 'add_category') {
        $name = trim($_POST['name'] ?? '');
        $icon = trim($_POST['icon'] ?? 'ri-mail-line') ?: 'ri-mail-line';
        $sort = (int)($_POST['sort_order'] ?? 1);
        if ($name === '') jsonResponse(['success' => false, 'error' => 'Category name required.']);

        $slug = makeSlug($name);
        $chk  = $pdo->prepare("SELECT id FROM categories WHERE slug = ?");
        $chk->execute([$slug]);
        if ($chk->fetch()) jsonResponse(['success' => false, 'error' => "Category '$slug' already exists!"]);

        $pdo->prepare("INSERT INTO categories (name, slug, icon, sort_order) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)")
            ->execute([$name, $slug, $icon, $sort]);
        jsonResponse(['success' => true, 'message' => "Category '$name' created!"]);
    }

    if ($act === 'delete_category') {
        $id = (int)($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        $pdo->prepare("DELETE FROM categories WHERE id = ?")->execute([$id]);
        jsonResponse(['success' => true, 'message' => 'Category deleted!']);
    }

    // 7. PRODUCTS (WITH SHORT & FULL DESCRIPTION)
    if ($act === 'add_product') {
        $name     = trim($_POST['name'] ?? '');
        $category = strtolower(trim($_POST['category'] ?? 'mail'));
        $price    = max(0.01, (float)($_POST['price'] ?? 1.00));
        $desc     = trim($_POST['description'] ?? '');
        $fullDesc = trim($_POST['full_description'] ?? '');
        if ($name === '') jsonResponse(['success' => false, 'error' => 'Product name required.']);

        $slug = makeSlug($name);
        $chk  = $pdo->prepare("SELECT id FROM products WHERE slug = ?");
        $chk->execute([$slug]);
        if ($chk->fetch()) jsonResponse(['success' => false, 'error' => "Product '$slug' already exists!"]);

        $pdo->prepare("INSERT INTO products (name, slug, category, price, description, full_description) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)")
            ->execute([$name, $slug, $category, $price, $desc, $fullDesc]);
        jsonResponse(['success' => true, 'message' => "Product '$name' created!"]);
    }

    if ($act === 'update_product') {
        $id       = (int)($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        $name     = trim($_POST['name'] ?? '');
        $category = strtolower(trim($_POST['category'] ?? 'mail'));
        $price    = max(0.01, (float)($_POST['price'] ?? 1.00));
        $desc     = trim($_POST['description'] ?? '');
        $fullDesc = trim($_POST['full_description'] ?? '');
        $pdo->prepare("UPDATE products SET name = ?, category = ?, price = ?, description = ?, full_description = ? WHERE id = ?")
            ->execute([$name, $category, $price, $desc, $fullDesc, $id]);
        jsonResponse(['success' => true, 'message' => 'Product updated!']);
    }

    if ($act === 'toggle_product') {
        $id = (int)($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        $pdo->prepare("UPDATE products SET status = IF(status='active','inactive','active') WHERE id = ?")->execute([$id]);
        jsonResponse(['success' => true, 'message' => 'Status toggled!']);
    }

    if ($act === 'delete_product') {
        $id = (int)($_POST['id'] ?? 0);
        $pdo->prepare("DELETE FROM products WHERE id = ?")->execute([$id]);
        jsonResponse(['success' => true, 'message' => 'Product deleted!']);
    }

    // 8. STOCK UPLOAD
    if ($act === 'upload_stock') {
        $productId = (int)($_POST['product_id'] ?? 0);
        $rawText   = trim($_POST['stock_text'] ?? '');
        $tgUrl     = trim($_POST['tg_url'] ?? '');
        if ($productId <= 0) jsonResponse(['success' => false, 'error' => 'Select a product first.']);

        if (!empty($_FILES['stock_file']['tmp_name'])) {
            $fc = file_get_contents($_FILES['stock_file']['tmp_name']);
            if ($fc !== false) $rawText .= "\n" . $fc;
        }
        if ($tgUrl !== '') {
            $uc = @file_get_contents($tgUrl);
            if ($uc === false) jsonResponse(['success' => false, 'error' => 'Failed to download URL.']);
            $rawText .= "\n" . $uc;
        }

        $lines = array_filter(array_map('trim', explode("\n", $rawText)), fn($l) => $l !== '');
        if (empty($lines)) jsonResponse(['success' => false, 'error' => 'No valid stock lines found.']);

        $stmt = $pdo->prepare("INSERT INTO stock (product_id, data) VALUES (?, ?)");
        $inserted = 0;
        foreach ($lines as $line) {
            $line = substr($line, 0, 1500);
            if ($line === '') continue;
            $stmt->execute([$productId, $line]);
            $inserted++;
        }
        jsonResponse(['success' => true, 'message' => "Uploaded $inserted items to stock!"]);
    }

    if ($act === 'clear_stock') {
        $productId = (int)($_POST['product_id'] ?? 0);
        $pdo->prepare("DELETE FROM stock WHERE product_id = ? AND status = 'available'")->execute([$productId]);
        jsonResponse(['success' => true, 'message' => 'Available stock cleared!']);
    }

    // 9. DEPOSITS & USERS
    if ($act === 'handle_deposit') {
        $depId    = (int)($_POST['deposit_id'] ?? 0);
        $decision = $_POST['decision'] ?? '';
        $pdo->beginTransaction();
        try {
            $stmt = $pdo->prepare("SELECT * FROM deposits WHERE id = ? FOR UPDATE");
            $stmt->execute([$depId]);
            $dep = $stmt->fetch();
            if (!$dep || $dep['status'] !== 'pending') {
                $pdo->rollBack();
                jsonResponse(['success' => false, 'error' => 'Already processed.']);
            }
            if ($decision === 'approve') {
                $pdo->prepare("UPDATE deposits SET status = 'approved', updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = ?")->execute([$depId]);
                $pdo->prepare("UPDATE users SET balance = balance + ? WHERE id = ?")->execute([$dep['amount'], $dep['user_id']]);
                $pdo->prepare("UPDATE payment_sms_logs SET is_used = 1, used_by_dep = ? WHERE trx_id = ?")->execute([$depId, strtoupper(trim((string)$dep['trx_id']))]);
            } else {
                $pdo->prepare("UPDATE deposits SET status = 'rejected', updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = ?")->execute([$depId]);
            }
            $pdo->commit();
            jsonResponse(['success' => true, 'message' => 'Deposit ' . $decision . 'd!']);
        } catch (Throwable) {
            if ($pdo->inTransaction()) $pdo->rollBack();
            jsonResponse(['success' => false, 'error' => 'Database error.']);
        }
    }

    if ($act === 'handle_api_access') {
        $userId = (int)($_POST['user_id'] ?? 0);
        $status = $_POST['api_status'] ?? 'none';
        if ($status === 'approved') {
            $stmt = $pdo->prepare("SELECT api_key FROM users WHERE id = ?");
            $stmt->execute([$userId]);
            $key = $stmt->fetchColumn() ?: generateApiKey();
            $pdo->prepare("UPDATE users SET api_status = 'approved', api_key = ? WHERE id = ?")->execute([$key, $userId]);
            jsonResponse(['success' => true, 'message' => 'API Access Approved!']);
        } else {
            $pdo->prepare("UPDATE users SET api_status = 'revoked' WHERE id = ?")->execute([$userId]);
            jsonResponse(['success' => true, 'message' => 'API Access Revoked!']);
        }
    }

    if ($act === 'adjust_balance') {
        $userId = (int)($_POST['user_id'] ?? 0);
        $amount = (float)($_POST['amount'] ?? 0);
        $pdo->prepare("UPDATE users SET balance = GREATEST(0, balance + ?) WHERE id = ?")->execute([$amount, $userId]);
        jsonResponse(['success' => true, 'message' => 'User balance updated!']);
    }

    if ($act === 'toggle_user_ban') {
        $userId = (int)($_POST['user_id'] ?? 0);
        $pdo->prepare("UPDATE users SET status = IF(status='active','banned','active') WHERE id = ?")->execute([$userId]);
        jsonResponse(['success' => true, 'message' => 'User status updated!']);
    }

    if ($act === 'get_deposit_screenshot') {
        $depId = (int)($_POST['deposit_id'] ?? 0);
        $stmt  = $pdo->prepare("SELECT id, method, sender_num, amount, trx_id, status, screenshot, created_at FROM deposits WHERE id = ? LIMIT 1");
        $stmt->execute([$depId]);
        $row = $stmt->fetch();
        if (!$row || empty($row['screenshot'])) {
            jsonResponse(['success' => false, 'error' => 'No screenshot found for this deposit.']);
        }
        jsonResponse(['success' => true, 'deposit' => $row]);
    }

    if ($act === 'save_settings') {
        $keys = [
            'bkash_status', 'bkash_number',
            'nagad_status', 'nagad_number',
            'binance_status', 'binance_id', 'binance_rate',
            'binance_api_key', 'binance_api_secret',
            'sms_sync_secret',
            'min_deposit', 'support_link', 'site_notice', 'google_client_id'
        ];
        $stmt = $pdo->prepare("INSERT INTO settings (setting_key, setting_value) VALUES (?, ?) ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE setting_value = VALUES(setting_value)");
        foreach ($keys as $k) {
            if (isset($_POST[$k])) $stmt->execute([$k, trim($_POST[$k])]);
        }
        if (function_exists('clearStoreCache')) clearStoreCache();
        jsonResponse(['success' => true, 'message' => 'Payment & Store Settings saved!']);
    }
}

$isAdmin = isAdminLoggedIn();
if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_ACTIVE) session_write_close();
$categories = $products = $users = $deposits = $orders = $settings = [];
$statStock = $statPendingDep = $statPendingApi = 0;
$statSalesToday = 0.0;

if ($isAdmin) {
    if (function_exists('runBackgroundPaymentMatcher')) {
        runBackgroundPaymentMatcher($pdo);
    }

    $categories = $pdo->query("SELECT * FROM categories ORDER BY sort_order ASC, id ASC")->fetchAll();
    $products   = $pdo->query("
        SELECT p.*, COUNT(s.id) AS stock
        FROM products p
        LEFT JOIN stock s ON s.product_id = p.id AND s.status = 'available'
        GROUP BY p.id
        ORDER BY p.category ASC, p.id DESC
    ")->fetchAll();
    foreach ($products as $p) $statStock += (int)$p['stock'];

    $users = $pdo->query("SELECT id, name, email, balance, api_key, api_status, status, created_at FROM users ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 200")->fetchAll();
    foreach ($users as $u) if ($u['api_status'] === 'pending') $statPendingApi++;

    $deposits = $pdo->query("
        SELECT d.id, d.user_id, d.method, d.sender_num, d.amount, d.trx_id, d.status, d.created_at,
               (d.screenshot IS NOT NULL AND d.screenshot != '') AS has_screenshot,
               u.name AS user_name, u.email AS user_email
        FROM deposits d
        LEFT JOIN users u ON u.id = d.user_id
        ORDER BY FIELD(d.status, 'pending', 'approved', 'rejected'), d.id DESC
        LIMIT 100
    ")->fetchAll();
    foreach ($deposits as $d) if ($d['status'] === 'pending') $statPendingDep++;

    $orders = $pdo->query("
        SELECT o.id, o.order_code, o.user_id, o.product_id, o.product_name, o.qty, o.unit_price, o.total_price, o.buy_source, o.created_at, u.email AS user_email
        FROM orders o
        LEFT JOIN users u ON u.id = o.user_id
        ORDER BY o.id DESC
        LIMIT 50
    ")->fetchAll();

    $statSalesToday = (float)$pdo->query("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(total_price), 0) FROM orders WHERE created_at >= CURDATE()")->fetchColumn();
    $settings = getSettings($pdo);
}

function renderCatIcon(string $ico): string {
    if (str_starts_with($ico, 'ri-')) return '<i class="' . htmlspecialchars($ico) . '"></i>';
    return '<i class="ri-apps-2-line"></i>';
}

// Prevents Cloudflare from turning emails into [email protected]
function cfSafeEmail(?string $email): string {
    $clean = htmlspecialchars((string)$email);
    return str_replace('@', '<span>&#64;</span>', $clean);
}

<script>
document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
  const btn = e.target.closest('.btn, .menu-btn, .dock-btn, .d-btn');
  if (!btn) return;
  const r = btn.getBoundingClientRect();
  const sp = document.createElement('span');
  const sz = Math.max(r.width, r.height);
  sp.style.width = sp.style.height = sz + 'px';
  sp.style.left = (e.clientX - r.left - sz / 2) + 'px';
  sp.style.top  = (e.clientY - r.top - sz / 2) + 'px';
  sp.className  = 'ripple-effect';
  btn.appendChild(sp);
  setTimeout(() => sp.remove(), 500);
});

function startLoad() { document.getElementById('topLoader').className = 'top-loader active'; }
function endLoad() {
  const l = document.getElementById('topLoader');
  l.className = 'top-loader done';
  setTimeout(() => l.className = 'top-loader', 280);
}

function toggleDrawer(open) {
  document.getElementById('drawer').classList.toggle('open', open);
  document.getElementById('drawerOv').classList.toggle('open', open);
}

function closeModal(id) { document.getElementById(id).classList.remove('show'); }

function openConfirmModal(title, message, onConfirmCallback) {
  document.getElementById('confTitle').innerHTML = `<i class="ri-alert-line"></i> ` + title;
  document.getElementById('confMessage').textContent = message;
  const yesBtn = document.getElementById('btnConfYes');
  yesBtn.onclick = () => {
    closeModal('confirmModal');
    onConfirmCallback();
  };
  document.getElementById('confirmModal').classList.add('show');
}

function goSec(id, updateUrl = true) {
  toggleDrawer(false);
  document.querySelectorAll('.sec').forEach(s => s.classList.remove('active'));
  document.querySelectorAll('.d-btn, .dock-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));

  const sec = document.getElementById('sec-' + id);
  if (sec) sec.classList.add('active');

  ['nav-', 'm-'].forEach(prefix => {
    const el = document.getElementById(prefix + id);
    if (el) el.classList.add('active');
  });

  if (updateUrl) {
    if (id === 'dashboard') {
      history.pushState(null, null, window.location.pathname);
    } else {
      history.pushState(null, null, '#' + id);
    }
  }

  window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
}

function syncAdminFromUrl() {
  const hash = location.hash.replace('#', '').trim();
  if (hash && document.getElementById('sec-' + hash) && hash !== 'dashboard') {
    goSec(hash, false);
  } else {
    goSec('dashboard', false);
  }
}

window.addEventListener('hashchange', syncAdminFromUrl);

async function manualReloadAdmin() {
  const ico = document.getElementById('adminReloadIco');
  if (ico) {
    ico.style.transition = 'transform 0.5s ease';
    ico.style.transform  = 'rotate(360deg)';
    setTimeout(() => { ico.style.transition = 'none'; ico.style.transform = 'none'; }, 520);
  }
  startLoad();
  await softSyncAdmin();
  endLoad();
  toast('Admin data refreshed!');
}

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  document.getElementById('splashScreen').classList.add('hide');
  localStorage.removeItem('mrms_admin_tab');
  syncAdminFromUrl();

  // Auto-sync Admin tables & counters when switching back to tab or every 15s (Skip on Settings!)
  async function autoLiveSyncAdmin() {
    if (document.hidden) return;
    const activeSec = document.querySelector('.sec.active');
    if (activeSec && activeSec.id === 'sec-settings') return;
    const activeTag = document.activeElement ? document.activeElement.tagName : '';
    if (['INPUT', 'TEXTAREA', 'SELECT'].includes(activeTag)) return; // Don't interrupt while admin is typing!
    const curSearch = document.getElementById('userSearchInput') ? document.getElementById('userSearchInput').value : '';
    await softSyncAdmin();
    if (curSearch && document.getElementById('userSearchInput')) {
      document.getElementById('userSearchInput').value = curSearch;
      filterAdminUsers(curSearch);
    }
  }

  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
    if (!document.hidden) autoLiveSyncAdmin();
  });
  window.addEventListener('focus', autoLiveSyncAdmin);
  setInterval(autoLiveSyncAdmin, 15000);
});

function toast(msg, err = false) {
  const t = document.getElementById('toast');
  t.style.background = err ? '#e11d48' : '#0f172a';
  t.innerHTML = `<i class="${err ? 'ri-error-warning-line' : 'ri-checkbox-circle-line'}"></i> ` + msg;
  t.classList.add('show');
  setTimeout(() => t.classList.remove('show'), 3200);
}

async function post(obj) {
  startLoad();
  const fd = new FormData();
  for (const k in obj) fd.append(k, obj[k]);
  try {
    const r = await fetch('admin.php', { method: 'POST', body: fd });
    endLoad();
    return await r.json();
  } catch (e) {
    endLoad();
    return { success: false, error: 'Network error' };
  }
}

function escCode(s) {
  return String(s ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, m => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[m]));
}

async function createCategory() {
  const d = await post({
    admin_action: 'add_category',
    name: document.getElementById('catName').value,
    icon: document.getElementById('catIcon').value,
    sort_order: document.getElementById('catSort').value
  });
  if (!d.success) return toast(d.error, true);
  toast(d.message);
  softSyncAdmin();
}

// Silent Background Sync: Updates all related dropdowns, tables & counters without reloading page!
async function softSyncAdmin() {
  try {
    const res  = await fetch('admin.php', { cache: 'no-store' });
    const html = await res.text();
    const doc  = new DOMParser().parseFromString(html, 'text/html');

    // Sync all related sections, dropdowns (<select>) & tables silently
    const syncIds = ['sec-dashboard', 'sec-categories', 'sec-products', 'sec-upload', 'sec-deposits', 'sec-users', 'sec-orders', 'drawer'];
    syncIds.forEach(id => {
      const curEl = document.getElementById(id);
      const newEl = doc.getElementById(id);
      if (curEl && newEl) {
        curEl.innerHTML = newEl.innerHTML;
      }
    });

    // Keep active menu button highlighted
    const activeSec = document.querySelector('.sec.active');
    if (activeSec) {
      const tabId = activeSec.id.replace('sec-', '');
      const navBtn = document.getElementById('nav-' + tabId);
      if (navBtn) navBtn.classList.add('active');
    }
  } catch (e) {}
}

// Helper to smoothly animate & remove a row AND sync all related dropdowns/tables!
function removeRowSmoothly(rowEl) {
  if (rowEl) {
    rowEl.style.transition = 'all 0.28s cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1)';
    rowEl.style.opacity    = '0';
    rowEl.style.transform  = 'translateX(24px) scale(0.96)';
    setTimeout(() => { rowEl.remove(); softSyncAdmin(); }, 280);
  } else {
    softSyncAdmin();
  }
}

function askDeleteCategory(id, name) {
  const btn = event ? event.currentTarget : null;
  const row = btn ? btn.closest('tr') : null;
  openConfirmModal('Delete Category', `Are you sure you want to delete category "${name}"?`, async () => {
    const d = await post({ admin_action: 'delete_category', id });
    if (!d.success) return toast(d.error || 'Failed', true);
    toast(d.message || 'Category deleted!');
    removeRowSmoothly(row);
  });
}

async function createProduct() {
  const d = await post({
    admin_action: 'add_product',
    name: document.getElementById('prodName').value,
    category: document.getElementById('prodCat').value,
    price: document.getElementById('prodPrice').value,
    description: document.getElementById('prodDesc').value,
    full_description: document.getElementById('prodFullDesc').value
  });
  if (!d.success) return toast(d.error, true);
  toast(d.message);
  softSyncAdmin();
}

function openEditProd(p) {
  document.getElementById('epId').value       = p.id;
  document.getElementById('epName').value     = p.name;
  document.getElementById('epCat').value      = p.category;
  document.getElementById('epPrice').value    = p.price;
  document.getElementById('epDesc').value     = p.description || '';
  document.getElementById('epFullDesc').value = p.full_description || '';
  document.getElementById('editModal').classList.add('show');
}

async function saveEditProd() {
  const d = await post({
    admin_action: 'update_product',
    id: document.getElementById('epId').value,
    name: document.getElementById('epName').value,
    category: document.getElementById('epCat').value,
    price: document.getElementById('epPrice').value,
    description: document.getElementById('epDesc').value,
    full_description: document.getElementById('epFullDesc').value
  });
  if (!d.success) return toast(d.error, true);
  closeModal('editModal');
  toast(d.message);
  softSyncAdmin();
}

async function toggleProd(id) {
  const btn = event ? event.currentTarget : null;
  const row = btn ? btn.closest('tr') : null;
  const d   = await post({ admin_action: 'toggle_product', id });
  if (!d.success) return toast('Failed', true);
  toast('Product status toggled!');
  if (row) {
    const badge = row.querySelectorAll('.badge')[1];
    if (badge) {
      const isNowActive = badge.textContent.trim() !== 'ACTIVE';
      badge.textContent = isNowActive ? 'ACTIVE' : 'INACTIVE';
      badge.className   = 'badge ' + (isNowActive ? 'b-ok' : 'b-warn');
    }
  }
}

function askDeleteProd(id, name) {
  const btn = event ? event.currentTarget : null;
  const row = btn ? btn.closest('tr') : null;
  openConfirmModal('Delete Product', `Permanently delete "${name}" and all of its available stock?`, async () => {
    const d = await post({ admin_action: 'delete_product', id });
    if (!d.success) return toast(d.error || 'Failed', true);
    toast(d.message || 'Product deleted!');
    removeRowSmoothly(row);
  });
}

async function uploadStock() {
  const pid = document.getElementById('upProdId').value;
  if (!pid) return toast('Select a product first!', true);

  const btn = document.getElementById('btnUp');
  btn.disabled = true; startLoad();

  const fd = new FormData();
  fd.append('admin_action', 'upload_stock');
  fd.append('product_id', pid);
  fd.append('stock_text', document.getElementById('upText').value);
  fd.append('tg_url', document.getElementById('upUrl').value);
  const f = document.getElementById('upFile');
  if (f.files.length) fd.append('stock_file', f.files[0]);

  try {
    const r = await fetch('admin.php', { method: 'POST', body: fd });
    const d = await r.json();
    endLoad(); btn.disabled = false;
    if (!d.success) return toast(d.error, true);
    toast(d.message);
    document.getElementById('upText').value = '';
    document.getElementById('upUrl').value  = '';
    f.value = '';
    softSyncAdmin();
  } catch (e) {
    endLoad(); btn.disabled = false;
    toast('Network error while uploading!', true);
  }
}

function askClearStock() {
  const pid = document.getElementById('upProdId').value;
  if (!pid) return toast('Select a product first!', true);
  openConfirmModal('Clear Stock', 'Delete all available stock for the selected product?', async () => {
    const d = await post({ admin_action: 'clear_stock', product_id: pid });
    if (d.success) toast(d.message);
    softSyncAdmin();
  });
}

async function handleDep(id, decision) {
  const d = await post({ admin_action: 'handle_deposit', deposit_id: id, decision });
  if (!d.success) return toast(d.error, true);
  toast(d.message);
  softSyncAdmin();
}

async function submitBalAdjust() {
  const uid = document.getElementById('balUserId').value;
  const val = document.getElementById('balAmountInput').value.trim();
  if (!val) return toast('Enter an amount!', true);
  closeModal('balModal');
  const d = await post({ admin_action: 'adjust_balance', user_id: uid, amount: parseFloat(val) });
  if (d.success) toast(d.message);
  softSyncAdmin();
}

function filterAdminUsers(q) {
  const term = String(q || '').toLowerCase().replace('@', ' ').trim();
  document.querySelectorAll('#adminUsersTable tbody tr.user-row').forEach(tr => {
    const hay = tr.getAttribute('data-search') || '';
    tr.style.display = (!term || hay.includes(term)) ? '' : 'none';
  });
}

  const d = await post({ admin_action: 'toggle_user_ban', user_id: uid });
  if (d.success) toast(d.message);
  softSyncAdmin();
}

async function viewDepScreenshot(depId) {
  startLoad();
  const d = await post({ admin_action: 'get_deposit_screenshot', deposit_id: depId });
  endLoad();
  if (!d.success || !d.deposit) return toast(d.error || 'Could not load screenshot', true);

  const dep = d.deposit;
  document.getElementById('scMetaBox').innerHTML = `
    <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:3px">
      <span><b>Method:</b> ${escCode(dep.method)}</span>
      <span><b>Amount:</b> <b style="color:var(--primary)">৳${Number(dep.amount).toFixed(2)}</b></span>
    </div>
    <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between">
      <span><b>Sender:</b> <span class="mono">${escCode(dep.sender_num || '-')}</span></span>
      <span><b>TrxID:</b> <span class="mono" style="font-weight:800">${escCode(dep.trx_id)}</span></span>
    </div>
  `;
  document.getElementById('scPreviewImg').src = dep.screenshot;

  const actBox = document.getElementById('scActionBtns');
  if (dep.status === 'pending') {
    actBox.innerHTML = `
      <button class="btn btn-success" style="flex:1;padding:8px" onclick="closeModal('screenModal');handleDep(${dep.id}, 'approve')"><i class="ri-check-line"></i> Approve Payment</button>
      <button class="btn btn-danger" style="flex:1;padding:8px" onclick="closeModal('screenModal');handleDep(${dep.id}, 'reject')"><i class="ri-close-line"></i> Reject</button>
    `;
  } else {
    actBox.innerHTML = `<button class="btn btn-outline" style="width:100%;padding:8px" onclick="closeModal('screenModal')">Close Preview (${String(dep.status).toUpperCase()})</button>`;
  }

  document.getElementById('screenModal').classList.add('show');
}

<?php
// ============================================================
//  MRMS UNIVERSAL CODE EDITOR & 2-STEP SMART PATCHER — editor.php
//  Standalone cPanel Code Updater (Works on MRMS Mart & Any Website!)
//  1-Click File Upload & Replace + 2-Step Highlight Patcher + Terminal
// ============================================================

declare(strict_types=1);

if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) {
    session_start();
}

// Automatically load config.php if present in the same folder; otherwise work standalone on any cPanel website!
if (file_exists(__DIR__ . '/config.php')) {
    require_once __DIR__ . '/config.php';
}

if (!defined('SITE_NAME'))  define('SITE_NAME', 'MRMS Code Patcher');
if (!defined('ADMIN_USER')) define('ADMIN_USER', 'admin');
if (!defined('ADMIN_PASS')) define('ADMIN_PASS', 'admin123');

function editorJsonResponse(array $data, int $code = 200): never {
    http_response_code($code);
    header('Content-Type: application/json; charset=utf-8');
    header('Cache-Control: no-store, no-cache, must-revalidate');
    echo json_encode($data, JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE);
    exit;
}

function isEditorLoggedIn(): bool {
    return !empty($_SESSION['admin_logged_in']) && $_SESSION['admin_logged_in'] === true;
}

// Auto-discover all editable code files in the current cPanel folder (Prioritizes MRMS files first, then any website files)
function getEditableFilesList(): array {
    $priorityLabels = [
        'index.php'  => 'Customer Storefront (index.php)',
        'admin.php'  => 'Admin Control Panel (admin.php)',
        'api.php'    => 'Master API Engine (api.php)',
        'db.php'     => 'Database & Schema (db.php)',
        'config.php' => 'Configuration (config.php)',
        'editor.php' => 'Standalone Code Editor (editor.php)',
    ];

    $files = [];
    foreach ($priorityLabels as $fn => $label) {
        if (file_exists(__DIR__ . '/' . $fn)) {
            $files[$fn] = $label;
        }
    }

    $allowedExts = ['php', 'html', 'htm', 'js', 'css', 'json', 'txt', 'xml', 'sql', 'htaccess', 'env'];
    $scan = @scandir(__DIR__);
    if (is_array($scan)) {
        foreach ($scan as $item) {
            if ($item === '.' || $item === '..' || isset($files[$item])) continue;
            $full = __DIR__ . '/' . $item;
            if (!is_file($full)) continue;
            if (str_ends_with($item, '.bak')) continue;
            $ext = strtolower(pathinfo($item, PATHINFO_EXTENSION));
            if ($item === '.htaccess' || $item === '.env' || in_array($ext, $allowedExts, true)) {
                $files[$item] = $item . ' (' . number_format((int)@filesize($full)) . ' B)';
            }
        }
    }

    return $files;
}

$editableFiles = getEditableFilesList();

if (isset($_GET['logout'])) {
    unset($_SESSION['admin_logged_in']);
    header('Location: editor.php');
    exit;
}

// ── 1-CLICK DOWNLOAD ALL UPDATED FILES (SINGLE .TXT BUNDLE OR .ZIP) ──
if (isset($_GET['export_all']) && isEditorLoggedIn()) {
    $filesToExport = array_keys($editableFiles);
    $mode = $_GET['export_all'];

    if ($mode === 'zip' && class_exists('ZipArchive')) {
        $tmpZip = tempnam(sys_get_temp_dir(), 'mrms_zip_');
        $zip = new ZipArchive();
        if ($zip->open($tmpZip, ZipArchive::OVERWRITE) === true) {
            foreach ($filesToExport as $fn) {
                $p = __DIR__ . '/' . $fn;
                if (file_exists($p)) $zip->addFile($p, $fn);
            }
            $zip->close();
            header('Content-Type: application/zip');
            header('Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="Website_All_Files_' . date('Y-m-d_H-i') . '.zip"');
            header('Content-Length: ' . filesize($tmpZip));
            readfile($tmpZip);
            @unlink($tmpZip);
            exit;
        }
    }

    header('Content-Type: text/plain; charset=utf-8');
    header('Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="Website_All_Files_' . date('Y-m-d_H-i') . '.txt"');
    foreach ($filesToExport as $fn) {
        $p = __DIR__ . '/' . $fn;
        if (file_exists($p)) {
            echo "--- START OF FILE {$fn} ---\n\n";
            echo file_get_contents($p) . "\n\n";
        }
    }
    exit;
}

$loginError = '';
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['admin_login'])) {
    $u = trim($_POST['username'] ?? '');
    $p = trim($_POST['password'] ?? '');
    if ($u === ADMIN_USER && $p === ADMIN_PASS) {
        $_SESSION['admin_logged_in'] = true;
        header('Location: editor.php');
        exit;
    }
    $loginError = 'Invalid Admin username or password!';
}

// ============================================================
//  CODE EDITOR & PATCHER AJAX API HANDLER
// ============================================================
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['admin_action'])) {
    if (!isEditorLoggedIn()) {
        editorJsonResponse(['success' => false, 'error' => 'Unauthorized Access'], 403);
    }
    if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_ACTIVE) session_write_close();
    $act = $_POST['admin_action'];

    // 1. DIRECT FILE UPLOAD & REPLACE
    if ($act === 'upload_replace_file') {
        $targetFile = basename(trim($_POST['target_file'] ?? ''));
        if ($targetFile === '' || !isset($editableFiles[$targetFile])) {
            editorJsonResponse(['success' => false, 'error' => 'Invalid target file selected.']);
        }
        if (empty($_FILES['code_file']['tmp_name'])) {
            editorJsonResponse(['success' => false, 'error' => 'Please choose a file from your device first!']);
        }

        $uploadedContent = file_get_contents($_FILES['code_file']['tmp_name']);
        if ($uploadedContent === false || strlen(trim($uploadedContent)) < 5) {
            editorJsonResponse(['success' => false, 'error' => 'Selected file is empty or unreadable!']);
        }

        $destPath = __DIR__ . '/' . $targetFile;
        if (file_exists($destPath)) @copy($destPath, $destPath . '.bak');

        $bytes = @file_put_contents($destPath, $uploadedContent, LOCK_EX);
        if ($bytes === false) editorJsonResponse(['success' => false, 'error' => 'Failed to write file on server.']);
        if (function_exists('opcache_invalidate')) @opcache_invalidate($destPath, true);

        editorJsonResponse([
            'success' => true,
            'message' => "$targetFile uploaded & replaced! (" . number_format($bytes) . " bytes)",
        ]);
    }

    // 2. QUICK FIND & REPLACE SNIPPET
    if ($act === 'find_replace_code') {
        $file    = basename(trim($_POST['filename'] ?? ''));
        $find    = (string)($_POST['find_text'] ?? '');
        $replace = (string)($_POST['replace_text'] ?? '');

        if (!isset($editableFiles[$file])) editorJsonResponse(['success' => false, 'error' => 'Invalid file selected.']);
        if (trim($find) === '') editorJsonResponse(['success' => false, 'error' => 'Please enter the code lines you want to find!']);

        $path = __DIR__ . '/' . $file;
        if (!file_exists($path)) editorJsonResponse(['success' => false, 'error' => 'File not found on server.']);

        $content     = (string)file_get_contents($path);
        $normContent = str_replace("\r\n", "\n", $content);
        $normFind    = str_replace("\r\n", "\n", $find);
        $normReplace = str_replace("\r\n", "\n", $replace);

        $count = substr_count($normContent, $normFind);
        if ($count === 0) {
            editorJsonResponse(['success' => false, 'error' => 'Exact code snippet not found in ' . $file . '!']);
        }

        @copy($path, $path . '.bak');
        $newContent = str_replace($normFind, $normReplace, $normContent);
        $written    = @file_put_contents($path, $newContent, LOCK_EX);

        if ($written === false) editorJsonResponse(['success' => false, 'error' => 'Failed to save changes!']);
        if (function_exists('opcache_invalidate')) @opcache_invalidate($path, true);

        editorJsonResponse([
            'success' => true,
            'message' => "Replaced $count match(es) in $file & saved!",
            'content' => $newContent
        ]);
    }

    // 3. LIVE CODE EDITOR: LOAD FILE
    if ($act === 'load_file') {
        $file = basename(trim($_POST['filename'] ?? ''));
        if (!isset($editableFiles[$file])) editorJsonResponse(['success' => false, 'error' => 'File not allowed.']);
        $path = __DIR__ . '/' . $file;
        if (!file_exists($path)) editorJsonResponse(['success' => false, 'error' => 'File not found.']);
        $content = file_get_contents($path);
        editorJsonResponse([
            'success'    => true,
            'filename'   => $file,
            'content'    => $content,
            'size'       => number_format(strlen((string)$content)) . ' bytes',
            'modified'   => date('Y-m-d H:i:s', (int)filemtime($path)),
            'has_backup' => file_exists($path . '.bak')
        ]);
    }

    // 4. LIVE CODE EDITOR: SAVE FILE
    if ($act === 'save_file') {
        $file    = basename(trim($_POST['filename'] ?? ''));
        $content = (string)($_POST['content'] ?? '');
        if (!isset($editableFiles[$file])) editorJsonResponse(['success' => false, 'error' => 'Not permitted.']);
        if (strlen(trim($content)) < 5) editorJsonResponse(['success' => false, 'error' => 'Code is empty!']);

        $path = __DIR__ . '/' . $file;
        if (file_exists($path)) @copy($path, $path . '.bak');

        $written = @file_put_contents($path, $content, LOCK_EX);
        if ($written === false) editorJsonResponse(['success' => false, 'error' => 'Write failed!']);
        if (function_exists('opcache_invalidate')) @opcache_invalidate($path, true);

        editorJsonResponse(['success' => true, 'message' => "$file saved! (" . number_format($written) . " bytes)"]);
    }

    // 5. RESTORE BACKUP
    if ($act === 'restore_backup') {
        $file = basename(trim($_POST['filename'] ?? ''));
        if (!isset($editableFiles[$file])) editorJsonResponse(['success' => false, 'error' => 'Invalid file.']);
        $path = __DIR__ . '/' . $file;
        $bak  = $path . '.bak';
        if (!file_exists($bak)) editorJsonResponse(['success' => false, 'error' => 'No backup found.']);
        @copy($bak, $path);
        if (function_exists('opcache_invalidate')) @opcache_invalidate($path, true);
        editorJsonResponse(['success' => true, 'message' => "Restored backup of $file!"]);
    }
}

$isAdmin = isEditorLoggedIn();
if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_ACTIVE) session_write_close();
$firstFile = array_key_first($editableFiles) ?: 'index.php';
